Each such debit is within S3-R21's per-term bound (at most PIN_MAX per term), but it falls on a term that can never land, and S3-R22 sizes LAND_RESERVE as two terms of exposure for terms that can land, so no sizing counts a stream of pins on unlandable terms **[assumed: reading of S3-R21 and S3-R22; restated 2026-10-03, red team round 1 recheck: this sentence first read "outside S3-R21's per-term exposure, which counts only terms that can land", which the adjacent case below contradicted]**. This closes the pin half of C4 §10's S1/S3 row (the no-preactivation-debit boundary) on C2's side. **[open: C2 with S3 and C4-R12, adjacent case, not a migration bridge]** After a CONFLICT restart, `lastLandedTerm = firstPermittedTerm − 1` passes over the terms from the old `lastLandedTerm + 1` to `firstPermittedTerm − 1`. Those terms never land, and inside ROLE_HORIZON they stay pin-payable at up to PIN_MAX each. By the reading above they stand where the pre-activation pins stood: each pin is within S3-R21's per-term bound, but the terms can never land, so S3-R22's sizing does not count them **[proven: from the premise above and C4-R07's restart assignment; earlier text read "That is within S3-R21's per-term exposure", corrected 2026-10-03]**. Whether to floor them at `firstPermittedTerm` is left to C2 and S3 (revision 2026-10-03, migration-bridge-01).

**[proven: every greedy landing is paid, conditional on V9, on C3's MAX_FI_RUN and on the FI record size, open until E02]** Every record fits one blob, so a greedily packed landing that is not the term's last carries more than LANDING_BYTES − 130,044 = 260,088 bytes even at 2-s slots, and with the record that did not fit it more than 390,132; at most about 12,500 bytes of a term go uncounted (framing, at most 40 FI records of about 250 bytes, the view changes they open). The 40 rests on C3-R08's hard cap, derived: an FI block is valid only if fewer than MAX_FI_RUN = 2 consecutive FI blocks precede it (C3-R08, a validity cap since D17), so at most 2 of every 3 blocks over the term's 60 are FI, 40; the about 250 bytes is the page's figure for a payload-free FI record and is not bounded until E02 fixes the `headerCore` widths (§14, §16). So after the k-th greedy landing `bytesLanded` exceeds (k − 2) × 260,088 + 377,000 > (k − 1) × 254,000 for every k ≥ 2, and every greedy landing is paid. A cap term takes at most five greedy landings at 2-s slots, two at 12-s slots, and any lander is paid at most ⌈1,270,000 / 254,000⌉ = 5 × r_land; splitting earns nothing beyond that. (Round 6, R6H-3.) **[open: C6 MP-05]** This proof covers landings ended by bytes; a landing split because a costed admission cap is reached (MP-05's proposed disposition) is not covered: five small views of one term under a one-view cap need five calls and this counter pays one. The condition, the maximum call count and the per-term exposure below are re-derived together if C2 adopts that disposition.

**[assumed: the per-term exposure, consumed by S3]** The most a term can debit is 60 × R_BLK_MAX + 15 × R_BLOB + 5 × R_LAND_MAX + 60 × ATT_REWARD_PER_BLOCK + PIN_MAX = 240 + 15 + 6,500 + 6 + 400 = 7,161 TAIKO, realised only at the maximum ramp; with R_BLOB capped at 15 blobs per term and r_land at five landings the sum bounds every landing pattern (sixty one-block landings would otherwise have earned 60 × R_BLOB, and two such terms would exceed LAND_RESERVE; round 6 fix pass). The break-even L1 fee at which a third party is paid in full is about 100 gwei per landing whatever the term size and slot time, since every greedy landing is paid and R_LAND_MAX prices one 2 M-gas landing at 100 gwei at the assumed rate; above it landing waits for the fee to fall or the holder to land at its own cost, and nothing is penalized meanwhile beyond C2-R16's two disclosed pin residuals, the late self-lander and the partial-landing front-run (revised 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 3 Lows; earlier "and nothing is penalized meanwhile."). (Landing §5, §11; round 4, R4H-8.)

### C2-R17. The reserve as the funding source

**[assumed: interface to S3]** Reward-type debits (ramps, blob reward, boost, per-landing reward, attester share, PIN_REWARD) are drawn from the holder's `reserveGwei`, LAND_RESERVE per seat required at registration and reactivation and funded by the owner's `fundReserve` (S3-R02; `deposit` credits the nonreserve balance only); MISS never touches it; a reward-type credit to an owner with seats refills that owner's reserve first, up to `seats × LAND_RESERVE`; the reserve leaves the ledger only through `requestExit + EVIDENCE_WINDOW`; an owner below one term of exposure per seat lapses (S1's suspension store) until a top-up plus `reactivate()`, and `ReserveLow` fires once per crossing below `(seats + 1) × 7,161`. C2 relies on invariant I2 as S3 states it (S3-R23): a drawn term's lander reward exists at landing time whatever the owner does, short of a class-A slash of itself and of the self-directed drain S3 costs as the residual (L20). Sizing, lapse line, hysteresis, outage arithmetic and residual are S3's. (Landing §5; round 4, R4H-3, R4-C2, R4-C4; round 5, R5S-4; round 6, R6S-2, R6H-2, R6H-3.) **Open (landing §5):** whether to index R_BLK_MIN to `block.basefee` through a DAO-set rate constant. Vector: E17 (§14).

## 8. The retention duty

### C2-R18. Who retains which bytes for how long

**[assumed: proposed rule]** The bytes of a certified block (signed header, sequencer signature, carried certificate (whose preimage the guest hashes under C2-R04's V3 clause, D23) or view change, assignment digest, execution payload, anchor hash) and its certificate are retained and served over `blocks_by_range` by every attester whose bit is in a certificate of that block, and by RPC nodes, for RETAIN_SECONDS after the block's timestamp; every attester that attested a block executed it (V6) and holds its bytes. Older landed blocks come from L1 blobs within the T2 window and from archive holders after it (A-ARCHIVE). The design adds no L1-accountable publication duty: a failure to serve is unprovable on L1, and an L1 duty would cost what the bonded-fragment path of #22188 costs (decomposition §4). What a failure means: if fewer than the Q attesters that certified a range serve it and nobody else holds it, the range never lands and after A1 is replaced from `lastLanded` with no penalty (limitation L3: Q + 1 silent colluders can certify data only they hold, an unslashable stall until the replacement window opens, outside T11); if anyone serves it, any party rebuilds the canonical blobs (C2-R02) and lands. Rate limits and request authentication are S2's. (Roles page §2; certificate page §7 W3, §9; after the #22188 review.) **Adversary schedule:** certify then delete (L3); a prover cartel (anyone with the bytes may prove, TH2); an archive that withholds (A-ARCHIVE is named, not enforced). Vector: E18 (§14).

**[assumed: chosen above the derived floor of 4,500 s]** RETAIN_SECONDS ≥ LAND_WINDOW_MAX + REPLACE_GRACE + LAND_CHAIN_GRACE = 4,500 s, the latest replaceable moment with the deferral measured from `termEnd(t')`; 7,200 s is a choice with unspecified slack above that floor, and this rule is the one normative statement of the duty (the source page's wording is superseded, §16 item 10; J's review of #22196 (2026-10-01), J-2). **Limitation [assumed]:** a landing stays possible after `replaceableFrom` until a replacement lands (C2-R09) and inside the horizon (C2-R10), up to about 34 h, but the duty to serve lapses at RETAIN_SECONDS; a prover returning from an outage longer than 2 h after the range's blocks depends on the holder, an RPC node or any other voluntary holder still having the bytes. Raising RETAIN_SECONDS to the landing horizon is the alternative not taken.

## 9. Origin authentication consumed by C1

### C2-R19. A-ORIGIN, A-ORIGIN-CHAIN, A-ORIGIN-PROGRESS

**[assumed: what C2 exports]** C1-R01 names A-ORIGIN: every selected origin `O_b` is authenticated against the intended L1 history and bound to L2 block `b`. For every block of a landed range: (a) binding, the hash sits in the L2 header, hence in `blockHash`, which PH names under the sequencer's signature (V1) and the guest proves by execution (V6), and its number is the signed `PH.anchorNumber`; (b) authentication, the guest witnesses the RLP of every distinct anchored L1 header, checks `keccak256(rlp) == parentBeaconBlockRoot` and `number == PH.anchorNumber` for each block anchoring it, chains them by parent hash to the journal's tip, and asserts C7's V5 (`L1[a].timestamp` within `[PH.timestamp − ANCHOR_MAX_AGE, PH.timestamp − ANCHOR_MIN_AGE]`, `anchorNumber` monotone, the genesis child's floor at C4's activation block, `activationL1Number` of C4-R07 under D25, no longer the drain block) as C7-R09 applies it (D14; J's review of #22199 (2026-10-01), J-1 and J-3); the inbox checks the tip against L1 history (L7). No L1 state root is checked: L2 receives one only through C1's reveal, which re-checks keccak over the full header. (Anchor page §1, §2; certificate page V5; issue #22147; TH17.)

**A-ORIGIN-CHAIN [proven, conditional on A-L1HIST and T3]:** all headers anchored by blocks of landed ranges lie on one parent-hash chain, L1's canonical chain at the newest landing, and equal numbers imply equal hashes. Within a range every anchored header chains to the tip, canonical at landing (L7). Across ranges each tip is at least the previous one and canonical when its landing is included; an L1 reorg replacing a block at or below an earlier tip also removes the L1 block containing that landing, which lies above its tip, and inbox state reverts with L1 (C2-R20). So in any L1 history containing both landings every anchored block is canonical and one number maps to one hash, which C1-R06's conflict case and the former Anchor rule A3 rest on.

**A-ORIGIN-PROGRESS [proven for the landed chain, conditional on V5 as C7-R09 applies it and on C3's inclusion bound]:** V5 makes the origin of every landed block at most ANCHOR_MAX_AGE older than that block, evaluated at every block against its own timestamp, and V4 makes L2 timestamps strictly increase. No block of any kind inherits a V5 verdict (C3-R07; C7-R09 at `0f0a2f0`; D41), so DRIFT_MAX = 0 (C3 §7, C7 §7): the origin of every landed block is at most ANCHOR_MAX_AGE = 1,800 s older than the block, and a signal in the state of an L1 block with timestamp `s` is in the origin's state of every landed L2 block with `PH.timestamp ≥ s + ANCHOR_MAX_AGE`; a producer that keeps a pre-signal origin longer fails V5 at every honest attester and is timed out (S2). A `pinCurrentOrigin()` forced through C3 therefore executes in a block whose origin contains every signal older than ANCHOR_MAX_AGE plus C3's inclusion bound (C3 §9). **Withdrawn (D41):** the inherited-verdict drift of the earlier text (2 s for an FI run under MAX_FI_RUN, up to 63 s for block `j` of a forced batch, composed to DRIFT_MAX = 65 s and a 1,865-s origin bound; D22 item 3), and the parked-producer argument that an FI child by inheritance keeps the stall clock running (D14, answering J's review of #22199 (2026-10-01), J-1); C7-R09 at `0f0a2f0` withdraws both, and after D39 no block must be FI, so a NORMAL child with a fresh anchor is valid under A-L1LIVE (C7 §8). An L1 stall longer than ANCHOR_MAX_AGE halts honest L2 production until L1 resumes (a consequence of V5, unchanged by D14). Before landing, a fake origin (possible only if Q attesters skipped V5) exists on an unlanded chain only, chains to no canonical tip, cannot land and is replaced; bridge withdrawals wait for "landed" and the Bridge quota bounds the residual (Anchor page §2, §13).

**[assumed: profile exported]** `l1AnchorTipNumber` is `uint48` and `anchorNumber` `u48`, matching C1's P-NUMBER. **Open (§16):** the guest's L1 header profile must equal C1's reveal parser profile (P-HEADER-BYTES, P-HEADER-FIELDS) so every landed origin is revealable; the pages state no guest header size bound.

## 10. Interface sketches

**[assumed: interface declaration]** The landing surface of the upgraded Inbox implementation behind the existing proxy; C8 indexes it. Registry, slashing, forced-inclusion and migration functions of the same interface belong to S1, S3, C3 and C4; the S2 structs `Certificate`, `ViewChange`, `RedrawProof`, `Replace` are referenced, not redefined.

```solidity
/// @custom:security-contact security@taiko.xyz
interface IEtnaInbox /* landing surface */ {
    struct Segment { uint32 termId; uint8 view; address sequencer; uint64 firstHeight; uint64 lastHeight; uint16 fiCount; }
    struct RewardBoost { uint32 termId; uint64 minRewardPerBlockGwei; bytes seqSig; }
    struct LandInput {
        uint64 firstHeight; uint64 endHeight; bytes32 parentBlockHash; bytes32 endBlockHash; bytes32 endStateRoot; bytes32 endPhHash;
        bytes32 endParentPhHash;  // phHash of endHeight − 1; hashed into the journal and stored as lastLanded.parentPhHash (C2-R04, C2-R06)
        Segment[] segments; Certificate endCert; ViewChange[] viewChanges; RedrawProof[] redraws; bytes32[] blobHashes;
        uint48 l1AnchorTipNumber; bytes32 l1AnchorTipHash; uint48 l1AnchorTipTimestamp; // for a hatch, anchor A and T_A (C3-R07 check 5); hatchAnchorNumber removed 2026-10-04, L1-03
        uint48 fiHeadBefore; uint48 fiHeadAfter; uint16 fiVoidCount;   // C3-owned (C3-R06, D41); L8 checks them
        uint48[] fiReplayIds;     // C3-owned: at most MAX_FI_PER_LANDING strictly increasing ids below the head, each replayable; empty for a hatch
        uint48 fiParentTimestamp; // hatch (view 254) only, else zero and ignored; forced journal field (C3-R07 check 5); C3-owned
        RewardBoost[] boosts; Replace replace; uint48 deadline; address rewardTo; bytes proof; }
        // replace: derived (C2-R05 L4, CS-R4-01, 2026-10-04): the binding (last) REPLACE VC's l1Ref in viewChanges[] (C2-R05 L4), or the dead-mode object's L1 reference,
        // (earlier: "the consumed REPLACE VC's l1Ref"; red-team cycle 2 round 5, CS-R5-03, 2026-10-04; a landing may consume several REPLACEs, E23 (vii))
        // or ((c), D40(3), round 4 second recheck) the post-deadline, or scan-positive open-empty or holder-path, sentinel object's reference,
        // iff isReplace, else zero (a RESUME landing included); any other value reverts ViewChangeMismatch
    struct LastLanded { bytes32 blockHash; bytes32 stateRoot; bytes32 phHash; bytes32 parentPhHash; // parentPhHash: phHash of height − 1 (C2-R04, C2-R06)
                        uint64 height; uint32 termId; uint8 view; uint48 at; uint48 anchorTipNumber; uint48 anchorTipTimestamp; } // five words
    struct TermRecord { uint64 landedThrough; uint8 lastView; uint8 missBits; uint8 landingsPaid; uint8 blobsPaid;
                        uint32 bytesLanded; uint48 landedAt; uint48 landableFrom; uint8 flags; } // 29 bytes, one word
    struct ProvisionalRecord { uint64 startHeight; bytes32 startBlockHash; bytes32 startStateRoot; uint48 fiHeadAtStart;
                               bytes32 startDigest; // keccak256(abi.encode(lastLanded, lastVCHash)) at the landing; C2-R13, C2-R14's restart
                               uint64 endHeight; bytes32 endBlockHash; bytes32 endStateRoot; bytes32 journalHash;
                               uint8 leafId; uint48 landedAt; uint8 status; uint8 confirmingLeaf; } // seven words; PROVISIONAL, CONFIRMED, FINAL, CONFLICTED (C8-R06; CONFLICTED added 2026-10-04, L1-R3-01)
        // confirmingLeaf: set by confirmProvisional's confirm path, read by the finalization pass for ProvisionalFinalized; 0 until then (C2-R13; red-team cycle 2 round 5, L1-R5-02, 2026-10-04)
    struct Journal { /* the fields of C2-R04 in that order; each from the source C2-R04's partition names */ }

    function land(LandInput calldata _in) external;                              // anyone; C2-R05, C2-R06
    function landFor(address _rewardTo, LandInput calldata _in) external;        // msg.sender == G_LAND (C5)
    function recordViewChange(ViewChange calldata _vc) external;                 // REPLACE, RESUME per C2-R09; others per S2
    function announceLanding(uint64 _firstHeight, uint64 _endHeight) external;   // C2-R08; NotActive in CONFLICT (C2-R14, L1-R4-02)
    function recordAssignment(uint32 _term) external;                            // S1's pin; PIN_REWARD per C2-R16
    function confirmProvisional(uint32 _k, Journal calldata _recorded, Journal calldata _journal, bytes calldata _proof) external;
        // _recorded must hash to record k's journalHash; _journal == _recorded confirms (C2-R13), equal fixed inputs and a
        // different derived output is a conflict (C2-R14; the forced-inclusion fields are fixed inputs, C3-R06), else NotConflict; no reward
        // both paths: verify(keccak256(abi.encode(_journal)), _proof, 1) and the returned zkLeaves_ must name a ZK leaf
        // other than record k's leafId, else NotConflict; presented tags count for nothing (C2-R13, L1-01-R1, 2026-10-04)
        // runs C2-R13's finalization pass first, as a landing does, so a time-finalizable record is FINAL at check (1) (round 3 Lows)
        // both paths first: first <= _k < next and record k PROVISIONAL, else NotConflict (C2-R13 check (1), L1-R3-02);
        // in CONFLICT only a conflict with _k < conflictK, which marks k CONFLICTED and lowers conflictK, else NotActive (C2-R14, L1-R3-01);
        // a CONFLICTED _k already failed check (1) with NotConflict, so NotActive is raised only on a PROVISIONAL _k (check-order follow-up)
    function finalizeProvisional(uint32 _k) external;                            // C2-R13; in CONFLICT the pass stops before conflictK (L1-R3-01)
    function lastLanded() external view returns (LastLanded memory head_);
    function lastLandedTerm() external view returns (uint32 term_);
    function termRecord(uint32 _term) external view returns (TermRecord memory record_);
    function replaceableFrom() external view returns (uint48 at_);
    function rewardPerBlock(uint32 _term, uint48 _at) external view returns (uint64 gwei_);
    function provisionalRecord(uint32 _k) external view returns (ProvisionalRecord memory record_);
    function provisionalCursor() external view returns (uint32 first_, uint32 next_, bool conflict_, uint32 conflictK_); // conflictK_ added L1-R3-01; 0 unless conflict_
    function protocolParams() external view returns (bytes memory encoded_);     // keccak256 of it is protocolParamsHash

    event Landed(uint32 indexed termId, uint8 view, uint64 firstHeight, uint64 endHeight, address indexed lander,
                 bytes32 endPhHash, bytes32 endBlockHash, uint32 certBitmap, uint64 rewardGwei, uint8 zkLeavesUsed, bool replaced);
        // replaced = C2-R05 L4's isReplace, (a), (b) or (c) (CS-R4-01, 2026-10-04; (c) round 4 second recheck; earlier read from the lander-set replace)
    event LandingAnnounced(uint64 firstHeight, uint64 endHeight, address indexed announcer, uint48 replaceableFrom);
    event AnnouncementForfeited(uint64 firstHeight, address indexed announcer, address indexed to, uint64 gwei);
    event SingleProofMode(bool active);
    event ProofConflict(uint32 indexed k, uint8 leafA, uint8 leafB);
    event ProvisionalFinalized(uint32 indexed k, uint8 confirmingLeaf); // the stored confirmingLeaf if CONFIRMED (time-old included), 255 = type(uint8).max for a PROVISIONAL record final by the time rule (L1-R4-03, 2026-10-04; earlier 0, a valid ZK leaf id; stored source red-team cycle 2 round 5, L1-R5-02, 2026-10-04)
    // AttestersRewarded is S3's event, emitted inside land
}
interface IEtnaVerifier { // replaces ComposeVerifier / ZkRequiredVerifier (C2-R03)
    // _journalHash = keccak256(abi.encode(Journal)) as the inbox assembles it from the sources of C2-R04's partition;
    // no journal field reaches a leaf except through this hash (leaf input = hashPublicInputs(_journalHash, leaf, address(0), chainId))
    function verify(bytes32 _journalHash, bytes calldata _proof, uint8 _kRequired) external view returns (uint8 zkLeaves_);
        // D72: reverts BadProof unless _kRequired distinct presented ZK leaves accept (also _kRequired 0 or above ZK_N);
        // returns the ids of the presented leaves that accepted; unpresented leaves are not called; a codeless presented leaf rejects
    function imageSets(uint8 _leaf) external view returns (bytes32[] memory current_, bytes32[] memory previous_, uint48 previousValidUntil_);
        // D72: reverts UnknownLeaf for an id that is not a ZK leaf (the TEE leaf, or an id at or above ZK_N)
}
```

**[assumed: errors, storage, P2P]** Landing-path errors: `NotActive, NotNext, DeadlineExceeded, RangeTooLarge, NotHolder, BadCertificate, ViewChangeMismatch, FallbackBelowCertificate, AnchorTooOld, the forced-inclusion errors of C3-R06 and C3-R07 at `76c6150` (`FiRange, FiVoidNotExpired, FiGateUnmet, HatchNotPermitted, HatchNotBonded, HatchAnchorBeforeSave, HatchAnchorTooOld`; C3's, indexed by C8-R07; and `HatchSizeOutOfRange` (C3-R07 check 4, D72), which postdates `76c6150`), NotReplaceable, NotConflict (C2-R13, C2-R14), TermBeyondHorizon` (`ProofConflicted` removed by D72: CONFLICT is an accepted write and refused calls in CONFLICT raise `NotActive`) (C2-R05 L4, C2-R10), and the verifier's `BadProof` (L11, C2-R03) and `UnknownLeaf` (`imageSets`, C2-R03). **[assumed: arbiter answer, D72]** `TermBeyondHorizon`, `BadProof` and `UnknownLeaf` are added; each other name is bound to its check in C2-R05 and C2-R14; `ProvisionalPending` and `NotFinalizable` are withdrawn, C2-R13 stating no revert. Storage after the Inbox proxy's 43-slot gap: `lastLanded` (5 slots: four hashes, `parentPhHash` among them (D23), and one word packing the 31 bytes of `height`, `termId`, `view`, `at` and the two anchor-tip fields; the earlier figure of 3 slots undercounted), the term-record ring aliased onto Shasta's proposal-hash slot 254 keyed `termId % TERM_RING`, recorded view changes with the highest recorded certificate per key, pinned assignment digests and committee roots, the provisional ring (12 records of seven words, `startDigest` among them) and its cursor word (`first`, `next`, the CONFLICT flag and `conflictK`, 104 bits; `conflictK` added 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 3, L1-R3-01); C4 and C8 hold the layout table (interfaces page §5). P2P: `LandingIntent {firstHeight, endHeight, lander, etaSeconds, sig}` (advisory; B's landing-intent discovery, decomposition §2; it announces a submission, not a transfer of a proof to another recipient), `Proof {firstHeight, endHeight, journalHash, blobHashesRoot, rewardTo, leafId, proofBytes}` (submittable by anyone with its bound `rewardTo` and over its bound blobs, C2-R02; two leaves' proofs may come from different provers when they bind one journal), `RewardBoost`; S2 owns topics and envelope bounds.

## 11. State machines

**[proven: projections of the rules, not additional transitions]**

```text
Per certified block
  SEALED --cert, ≥ Q--> CERTIFIED --land() covering h in L1 block N--> LANDED --N+1 exists--> L1-SAFE --beacon finality--> L1-FINAL
  CERTIFIED --replacement lands, τ > replaceableFrom, or a hatch lands (in an open window, or after A1)--> VOID (no penalty; C2-R08, C2-R09, C3-R07)
  CERTIFIED --first term older than ROLE_HORIZON and never pinned, or anchor tip out of the EIP-2935 window--> UNLANDABLE, replaceable (C2-R10)
  LANDED --L1 reorg drops N--> CERTIFIED (re-land; no penalty; C2-R20)
Global proof mode
  NORMAL --τ − lastLanded.at > DEGRADE_AFTER--> SINGLE-PROOF (kRequired = 1 for certified landings; sentinel and hatch stay at ZK_K)
  SINGLE-PROOF --any landing that updates lastLanded.at (not a hatch, which preserves it)--> NORMAL
  NORMAL | SINGLE-PROOF --ProofConflict(k)--> CONFLICT --DAO upgrade names the sound leaf, rolls back if needed--> NORMAL
  CONFLICT --ProofConflict(k′), k′ < conflictK--> CONFLICT (conflictK lowered; C2-R14, L1-R3-01)
Provisional record k
  PROVISIONAL --other accepting leaf (a ZK leaf ≠ record.leafId in zkLeaves_ of verify(..., 1), C2-R13; presented tags count for nothing), same journal--> CONFIRMED ;  PROVISIONAL --DEGRADED_FINALITY, no conflict--> FINALIZABLE
  CONFIRMED | FINALIZABLE --every earlier record FINAL--> FINAL (one checkpoint per pass)
  PROVISIONAL --other accepting leaf (zkLeaves_ of verify(..., 1), C2-R13, C2-R14), same fixed inputs, another derived output, C3-R06 steps 1 and 3 passing (C2-R04, C2-R14 (c))--> CONFLICTED
              (global CONFLICT, conflictK = k; k and everything after it void)
  PROVISIONAL, k′ < conflictK, in CONFLICT --the same conflict predicate--> CONFLICTED (conflictK = k′; k′ and everything after it void)
  every edge from PROVISIONAL needs first ≤ k < next (C2-R13 check (1)); CONFIRMED, FINAL and CONFLICTED have no confirm or conflict edge (a call on one reverts NotConflict at check (1); a PROVISIONAL call refused by CONFLICT reverts NotActive at check (3))
  CONFIRMED | FINALIZABLE, in CONFLICT, k ≥ conflictK --> no edge to FINAL until the restart clears the ring (L1-R3-01, L1-R3-02, 2026-10-04)
Lander (any address)
  IDLE --cert(L) seen, r ≥ cost--> PROVE --proofs (own, or gossiped with rewardTo = me and over my blobs, C2-R02)--> SUBMIT (expiry frame) --Landed(rewardTo = me)--> PAID
  SUBMIT --Landed(other) | expiry--> IDLE (cost 0 under the primary shape)
  SUBMIT (a gossiped proof bound to another recipient) --Landed--> IDLE (the submitter paid gas, the bound recipient is paid)
```

Other objects: `lastLanded` advances only by an accepted landing and reverts only with L1 or by the CONFLICT reinitializer; the deferral flag goes unspent → spent by an announcement (defers) or consumed by a REPLACE or RESUME record (defers nothing), spent --record--> spent (no change; red-team cycle 2 round 5, L1-R5-01, 2026-10-04), consumed --record--> consumed, and resets with `lastLanded` (C2-R08, C2-R09; revised 2026-10-04, L1-R4-01, earlier "unspent → spent"); an announcement goes outstanding → returned, forfeited, or voided and refunded at a CONFLICT restart (C2-R08, C2-R14; L1-R4-02); a REPLACE/RESUME record goes absent → live → dead (C2-R09); an image set goes current → previous → retired (C2-R03).

## 12. Numeric register

**[assumed: rule-owned register]** C2's sole numeric definitions; C6 indexes them. Units: seconds, bytes, gas, TAIKO (formulas on assumed inputs: a term's revenue about 0.01 ETH, 1 TAIKO about 1.5·10⁻⁴ ETH, limitation L15) or counts; nothing in L1 slots. "Quoted" marks a value another section owns that a C2 rule reads.

| ID | Value / unit | Derivation, rationale and status |
|---|---|---|
| MAX_BLOCKS / MAX_VIEWS / MAX_BLOBS | 256 / 4 / 6 per landing | **assumed:** bounds guest work; four views amortize fixed gas; six is L1's per-transaction blob cap. |
| MAX_FI_PER_LANDING | 64 per landing | **quoted (C3 §7 at `76c6150`):** consumed entries per landing, also the bound on `fiReplayIds` and `fiClear`'s batch; walk about 134k gas plus about 2.9k per entry (assumed, C3). |
| Blob usable bytes | 130,044 | **open:** stated as today's coder's figure; the page's "31 bytes per field element" gives 4,096 × 31 = 126,976, while 130,044 equals 254-bit packing of 4,096 elements less a 4-byte header. Fix before the vectors (§16). |
| V9 record bound | 130,035 bytes | **quoted (C7), derived:** 130,044 − 9 framing bytes (round 6, R6H-3). |

## 13. Argument and adversary coverage

**[proven: landed equals certified, conditional on A-CERT and A-EXEC]** L5 binds the end hash to a certificate and the guest chains every signed header from `lastLanded` to it (C2-R04): V7 of C7 by construction (TH4). **[proven: no state root without the required proofs, conditional on T3, A3 and A-L1HIST]** By C2-R04 and C2-R15. **[proven: nothing voided without a landed replacement inside the horizon]** By C2-R07, C2-R09, C2-R10, with S2's FALLBACK gates (round 5, R5S-5; round 6, R6S-3); beyond the horizon, void by time (L14); after a one-leaf landing, a DAO resolution can void it and its descendants (L-PF). Finality lag under sequencer collusion is bounded by LAND_WINDOW_MAX + REPLACE_GRACE + LAND_CHAIN_GRACE = 4,500 s after the first unlanded term's end, the deferral being spendable at most once per range (C2-R08; L3's "up to 75 minutes with a landing announcement"). **[proven: no accusation object on the landing path]** The only debit is MISS, once per `(t, v)` by a Q-signed TIMEOUT VC the landing consumes (S2, S3); no rule infers fault from a missing landing (I5; TH8). **[proven: no third party can make a winner revert after payment]** Every check depends on readable state and the winner's calldata; FI fees and refunds are pull-based; an entry saved after the proof passes L8 (TH11).

| Attack trace [assumed: adversarial schedule] | Result under the cited rules, or explicit limit |
|---|---|
| TH1: a forged proof from one leaf. | Two leaves in normal mode (C2-R03); in single-proof mode, for a false root, also a colluding quorum and no honest proof of the other leaf for a day; a false forced-inclusion disposition needs no quorum, only no timely sound counterproof, and its landing-time money is not restored (C2-R14's scoped paragraph, B-W22-A02, 2026-10-03); a journal with the record's fixed inputs and any other derived output halts landing (C2-R04's partition, C2-R14). Not defended: a common-mode guest bug (L7). |
| TH2, W6: a prover cartel refuses to prove. | The holder lands itself; otherwise the ramp attracts anyone with the certified data (C2-R18); an outage delays, never voids (C2-R07). Past RETAIN_SECONDS this is conditional on a voluntary holder of the bytes (C2-R18 limitation). |
| TH5, W1/W3/W5: withheld blocks or seal. | Uncertified blocks are unlandable (L5); provers rebuild blobs from committee-held data (C2-R02, C2-R18); no seal object. Residual: withholding inside the window costs latency only. |
| TH7: a landing skips the current forced request once its window is open. | L8's gate reverts (`FiGateUnmet`, C3-R06); no penalty; anyone may land a hatch that executes it (C3-R07, HATCH_BOND = 0, D51(3)); the bound is C3 §9's. |
| TH11, TH13: landing races, L1 congestion. | A race is free under the primary shape, and without the gate costs C5-R15's D-FLOOR(L) plus its own blob fees (C2-R20, D72 7a) **[assumed: arbiter answer, D72]**; windows in seconds; under 2.7 M gas for a pinned term (open: §12, MP-05); pinning paid up to §12's fees, else carried by the first landing; a third party paid in full up to about 100 gwei, above which the wait costs no bond. |
| TH12: an L1 reorg drops a landing or an anchored block. | The range returns to CERTIFIED and is re-landed (C2-R20); a reorg deeper than ANCHOR_MIN_AGE makes an honest range unlandable, replaced after A1, the holder losing that range's revenue (L11). |
| TH16, TH17, TH20, TH25. | The gate shapes fail closed (C5); the tip is checked on L1 (L7); image overlap in seconds (C2-R03); a mismatched guest is unlandable, liveness only (C2-R04). |
| Reorg of a landable backlog after a stall (round 2, S3; round 4, R4-C3). | Bounded, not prevented: once A1 holds the holder may fork and anyone may land a hatch (C3-R07); first to land wins; a hatch in an open window may land before A1 (D51(4)). Defences: no penalty; one bonded deferral per range (C2-R08); a REPLACE or RESUME vote on a canonical L1 reference at which A1 (or, for RESUME, the hatch-tip route) fails is class B (S3d); a reference reorged out or never canonical is no offence (revision 2026-10-03, slashing-01; earlier: "a false L1 reference is class B (S3d)"). Residual: one backlog reorg per stall (L6); poison content no guest can prove is likewise replaced (client blacklist open, landing §18). |
| Degraded mode: a false root checkpointed after 24 h. | Needs one ZK bug, a colluding quorum and the other leaf unusable for the window; the checkpoint is then as final as any other (L-PF residual; §16 to C1). |
| B-C2-04: an adversary opens single-proof mode while one leaf still works. | Possible by design at ZK_K = 2, not an exploit: the mode opens exactly when a funded lander cannot obtain ZK_K proofs for DEGRADE_AFTER, whichever of the four conditions of C2-R12 failed (a leaf, the bytes, a certificate, L1 inclusion); withholding one leaf opens it. The consequence is bounded by the provisional record, not by the timer: the one-leaf landing writes no checkpoint for DEGRADED_FINALITY and a false root still needs the colluding quorum of the row above (C2-R12, C2-R13; L-PF). |
| B-C2-01: a prover's proof for Alice is copied by Bob with Bob's recipient. | Verification fails: `rewardTo` is a journal field (C2-R04), so the copy is a different journal with no proof (round 2, S6). Bob may submit Alice's proof unchanged, paying the gas while Alice is paid (C2-R02). Residual: none on the landing path; the self-landing exclusion of C2-R16 keys on the bound recipient. |
| B-C2-02: a sequencer signs one PH twice and two landers carry different witnesses. | Not equivocation (S2-R03: one PH, one `phHash`); both blobs land the same state and the guest accepts either (C2-R02, C2-R04); the first included wins and the second fails L1 (C2-R09). Residual: a proof binds `blobHashesRoot`, so a second witness in circulation may cost a second proof (C2-R02's limitation; vector E09). The same holds for a second quorum witness of a view change the range carries: one logical id `vcHash`, one PH, one journal but for `blobHashesRoot` (D21, D23). |
| B's re-verdict on #22196 (2026-10-01, comment 5934018060), Medium: a holder and a quorum land a block whose carried certificate names a block off its ancestor line, or whose `carriedCertHash` is checked against the ancestor's `phHash` directly. | C2-R04's V3 clause (D23): keccak of the carried certificate's preimage equals `PH.carriedCertHash` and its inner `C.phHash` equals the ancestor's `phHash` at `carriedCertHeight`, else no valid proof, the ancestors at the start of a range decided against the journal's `parentPhHash` and `grandparentPhHash` (storage, the latter a previous landing's proven `endParentPhHash`), so a certificate of a sibling envelope of `lastLanded`'s parent (one block hash, another `phHash`, S2-R03) fails whatever range carries it; the first two blocks under an opening (C7-R06, same-view REPLACE and RESUME reopenings included, decided by `n − lockHeight ∈ {1, 2}` from the opening's witnessed logical fields) check `PH.carriedCertHash = PH.vcHash`, and the blocks of view 0 of `t_0` after GENESIS_CHILD, whose `PH.vcHash` is the genesis child's value and names no opening, fall under the certificate case with no opening witness; FORCED, sentinel-255 and GENESIS_CHILD blocks skip the clause (C7-R04). Residual: the quorum and signature checks of the carried certificate stay outside the guest, closed by accuser-supplied evidence where the preimage was published (C7-R15; D22 item 6); vector E11. |
| B's W12 verdict on #22196 (2026-10-02, comment 5945458948), High: in single-proof mode a faulty leaf proves a two-block range with false `endBlockHash`, `endStateRoot`, `endPhHash` and `endParentPhHash`, a colluding quorum certifies the false end, and the honest proof, differing in four fields, is refused as a conflict, so the false root is published after DEGRADED_FINALITY. | C2-R04's partition and C2-R14's predicate: every derived output may differ, every fixed input must be equal, the forced-inclusion fields among them (C3-R06), so the honest counterproof is a conflict and the record never finalizes; a sound leaf cannot raise a false conflict, since no guest answer about the queue enters a derived output under D41. Residual: a range the sound guest rejects outright has no counterproof (C2-R14, T11, L-PF). Vector E12. |
| B's W12 verdict on #22196, Medium: `endParentPhHash` has no calldata source for a multi-block range. | `LandInput.endParentPhHash` (§10), hashed into the journal and stored as `lastLanded.parentPhHash` as the same word (C2-R04, C2-R06); the guest proves it. Residual: none. |
| CONFLICT and the DAO never acts. | Every landing path waits (L-PF, beside L19); no objective rule between two valid proofs exists. Outside CONFLICT nothing waits for the DAO. |
| J-1 (#22196): the rollback re-lands a height holding a record, and C1-R06's first-write-wins reverts it. | Unreachable: the heights above the restored checkpoint were never written (C2-R14's restart claim, by C2-R13 and C2-R15), and no landing or pass writes a height twice (C2-R15); the rollback upgrade's epoch floors (C2-R21) keep it unreachable if those rules change. Residual: a bug in the inbox's sequence check, or a rollback upgrade that ignores C2-R21. |
| B's P-B-C1-03 (#22195 at 75616d5): (a) decreasing floors read the wrong epoch; (b) a rollback hides records but not the Bridge effects executed against them. | (a) C1-R06 owns the lookup and C2-R21 cites it: the last appended floor strictly below the queried height, epoch 1 when none, so a read at a floor height returns the restored record (vector E06); C2's floors are nondecreasing (C2-R21, proven), and C1's V12 decreasing-floor case tests the lookup for a hypothetical later upgrade only. (b) C2-R14's limitation: no such record is readable on C2's path (the restart claim); outside it, by case: a bug in C2-R13 or C2-R15 exposes one DEGRADED_FINALITY window of withdrawals at the Bridge quota plus the quota over the DAO's latency, unmeasured; a DAO rollback below a published root exposes the quota over the time since the oldest rolled-back checkpoint was written, open and set by the DAO's choice, which C2-R21 forbids; C4's table states what the reinitializer clears. |

**[assumed: failure profile]** All sequencers offline: anyone lands certified ranges; nothing voided without a landed replacement. All attesters offline: nothing landable, no deadline runs. All provers and landers offline: ranges accumulate CERTIFIED; the first returning prover clears the backlog at the maximum ramp only for the terms its first landing reaches (round 6, R6H-2), provided the bytes are still held, which C2-R18 guarantees only for RETAIN_SECONDS (a limitation, §16); past the horizon the backlog is replaced. L1 builders boycott: landing stalls without the gate, voids nothing (L13). Accepted false positives (landing §14): a guest bug costs the holder the term's revenue; a prover two hours behind a release is refused; an idle chain degrades harmlessly.

## 14. Examples and test-vector obligations

**[proven: arithmetic examples]** (a) A record of 130,035 bytes gives a payload of 1 + 4 + 4 + 130,035 = 130,044 bytes, one blob. (b) At 2-s slots four greedy landings of a cap term carry more than 4 × 260,088 = 1,040,352 of at most 1,282,500 bytes, so a fifth is needed at most, and ⌈1,270,000 / 254,000⌉ = 5 pays all. (c) A1 with `termEnd(t') = T`, `lastLanded.at = T − 1,500`: `max(T + 1,800, T − 900) = T + 1,800`; `min(T + 3,600, T + 1,800) + 300 = T + 2,100`; with the deferral spent by an announcement, T + 2,700; an accepted REPLACE or RESUME record consumes the flag and leaves T + 2,100 (revised 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 4, L1-R4-01; earlier "with the deferral spent, T + 2,700", read as covering a record too). (d) A proof race with a fixed recipient (C2-R02): prover P makes two-leaf proofs over range `[h + 1, h + 40]` with `rewardTo = A` and gossips them; A and B both submit them in the same L1 block, A's transaction first. A's landing is accepted and pays A; B's fails L1 (`NotNext`) and, as a direct land, pays C5-R15's D-FLOOR(L) plus its own blob fees (C2-R20, D72 7a) **[assumed: arbiter answer, D72]**, free under the primary shape (C2-R20). If B instead submits with `rewardTo = B`, the journal differs and L11 fails: B needs its own proofs. If B holds a blob whose `seqSig` of one block is the sequencer's second signature of the same PH (C2-R02), B's `blobHashesRoot` differs, P's proofs do not cover B's blobs, and B needs its own proofs over its own bytes; whichever landing is included first wins, and both end at the same `endBlockHash` and `endStateRoot`.

**[open: specification fixtures; owners C2/A and C8/A, with B contributing encoding review]** The E01 to E23 rows are fixture obligations (revised 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 3 Lows; earlier "The E01 to E19 rows", which left E20 to E22 outside the obligation; E23 added 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 4), not reports of executed tests; E13 to E19 close the vector gaps C8-traceability §2.2 names (C2-R03, C2-R07, C2-R10, C2-R11, C2-R17, C2-R18, C2-R20), each row naming its rules, and C8-traceability §3.1's three fixture classes apply: a row's logical projection is supplied now; its full deterministic encoding (argument encodings, error selectors, state) is owed now by this section **[open: specification fixture]** (for example E13 (xii)'s `imageSets(2)` call and `UnknownLeaf` selector); only bytes that depend on an implementation or a real proof are **[open: fixture after code]**. Closure requires exact inputs, expected encodings/state/errors and a rule-to-vector mapping, including the identity, restart and profile dependencies below. D36 and subsequent synchronization merges did not supply these artifacts; implementation runs are outside D2. A owns the D44 item-3 traceability work assigned in [5968122322](https://github.com/taikoxyz/taiko-mono/pull/22191#issuecomment-5968122322).

| Vector | Content | Status |

At `t_j + 86,401`, `finalizeProvisional(j)` finalizes nothing and writes nothing, with no revert (C2-R13), and no checkpoint at `j`'s end. The restart restores from `r = j`, the record at `first`. Control: without the second conflict, the same call finalizes `j` and writes `k`'s start, `j`'s false end; that is L-PF's untimely-counterproof case, not a defect. (ii) After (i)'s first conflict only (`conflictK = k`, record `k` CONFLICTED, record `j` PROVISIONAL). `confirmProvisional(k, J_k, J_k, proofB′)`, the confirm path on the CONFLICTED record `k`: `NotConflict` from check (1). A second conflict on `k`: `NotConflict` from check (1). The confirm path on the PROVISIONAL record `j`, `confirmProvisional(j, J_j, J_j, proofB′)`: `NotActive` from check (3). Variant, on its own timeline (re-timed 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 4, XS-E22-M-TIMING) **[proven: arithmetic on C2-R12 and C2-R14]**: leaf B stays down past `t_k + 100`; a third one-leaf record `m = k + 1` lands through A at `t_m = t_k + 7,300 = t_j + 14,600` (C2-R12 needs `t_m − t_k > DEGRADE_AFTER = 7,200`), so `next = m + 1`; B recovers and conflicts `k` at `t_m + 100 = t_j + 14,700`, giving `conflictK = k` with record `j` and record `m` PROVISIONAL; then a conflict on `m` (`m > conflictK`) gives `NotActive`, and so does the confirm path on `m`; a second conflict on `j` at `t_j + 86,000`, below `t_j + 86,400`, still lowers `conflictK` to `j` as in (i). (Earlier: "Variant with a third one-leaf record `m = k + 1` landed through A before the first conflict (`next = m + 1`), PROVISIONAL", on (i)'s timeline, where the first conflict at `t_k + 100` precedes every admissible one-leaf landing of `m` (at least `t_k + 7,201`) and CONFLICT then refuses landings, so the pre-state was unreachable.) After (i)'s second conflict as well (`conflictK = j`), a third conflict on `k`, now an older conflicted index above `conflictK`: `NotConflict` from check (1). Each call writes nothing. (Revised 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 3, L1-R3-01 check-order follow-up; earlier: "the confirm path: `NotActive`. A second conflict on `k` (`k ≥ conflictK`): `NotActive`. A record `m` with `k < m < next`, PROVISIONAL, conflict on `m`: `NotActive`", the first two contradicting check (1)'s order and `m` absent from the setup's `next = k + 1`.) (iii) In normal mode, no CONFLICT. Record 5 is FINAL with its checkpoint published and `first = next = 6`. `confirmProvisional(5, J_5, J′, proofB)`, with `J′` equal to `J_5` in every fixed input, a different `endStateRoot`, and B accepting: `NotConflict`, no CONFLICT, nothing written. The same call with `J′ = J_5`: `NotConflict`, record 5 stays FINAL. (iv) `k ≥ next`: `next = 18`, `first = 17`, slot `18 % 12 = 6` holding the FINAL record 6. `confirmProvisional(18, J_6, J′, proof)`: `NotConflict`; slot 6 is not read as record 18. (v) Record `k` CONFIRMED (leaf A landed, leaf B confirmed). A conflict over `J′` that leaf B also accepts: `NotConflict`, no CONFLICT. A second confirmation of `J_k`: `NotConflict`. (vi) The order of checks: a CONFLICT-state call on a FINAL record gives `NotConflict` from check (1), not `NotActive`; so does one on a CONFLICTED record, as in (ii) (added 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 3, L1-R3-01 check-order follow-up). (vii) An unsound counterproving leaf against true records (added 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 3 Lows). Records `j` and `k = j + 1` true, one-leaf through A; leaf B unsound; `first = j`. At `t_k + 100` B conflicts `k`: CONFLICT, `conflictK = k`. At `t_j + 86,000` B conflicts `j`: `ProofConflict(j, A, B)`, `conflictK = j`. `j` never finalizes; the restart restores from `r = j` and rolls back `j` and `k`, one record more than the first conflict alone; no checkpoint is written at `j`'s or `k`'s end; the DAO holds two pairs of journals with equal fixed inputs accepted by A and B. (viii) A time-finalizable record no pass has reached (added 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 3 Lows). Normal mode, `first = k`, record `k` true, one-leaf, PROVISIONAL, no landing or `finalizeProvisional` since `landedAt + 86,400`. At `landedAt + 86,401`, `confirmProvisional(k, J_k, J′, proofB)`, B unsound: the call's pass finalizes `k`, check (1) fails, `NotConflict`; the call reverts with its pass, and no CONFLICT is entered. `finalizeProvisional(k)` then finalizes `k`. Control: at `landedAt + 86,399` the same call raises `ProofConflict(k, A, B)`, the window as before. | **open** |
| E23 replacement classification, deferral consumption, announcements across CONFLICT, the time-rule sentinel (C2-R05 L4, L9, C2-R08, C2-R09, C2-R13, C2-R14, C3-R06 step 7; added 2026-10-04, red-team cycle 2 round 4, CS-R4-01, CS-R4-02, L1-R4-01, L1-R4-02, L1-R4-03) | (i) CS-R4-01: `lastLanded = (100, ph100)`, A1 holds; a landing with `viewChanges = [REPLACE(closes X, lock (100, ph100), l1Ref (N0, h0))]`, blocks B101..B110 and `endCert = C(B110)`: with `replace = (0, 0)` it reverts `ViewChangeMismatch` at L4 and writes nothing; with `replace = (N0, h0)` it passes L4 with `isReplace`, L9 tests A1, and on acceptance `Landed.replaced = true`, C3-R06 step 7 sets `hatchL1 = N` and `hatchTip[N] = keccak256(abi.encode(110, ph110))`, and an outstanding announcement on 101 is forfeited 40 % / 60 %. (ii) CS-R4-02: announcer A's bond outstanding on 101; after A1 an ordinary landing of 101..140 with no REPLACE VC and `replace = (1, 0x01)`: `ViewChangeMismatch`; the same with `replace = (0, 0)`: accepted, `isReplace` false, L9 not applied, A's bond returned, `Landed.replaced = false`, no `hatchL1` or `hatchTip` write. A RESUME landing (`viewChanges = [RESUME(..., l1Ref (N1, h1))]`) with `replace = (N1, h1)`: `ViewChangeMismatch`; with `replace = 0`: accepted before A1 when it follows a window hatch, `isReplace` false. (iii) A REPLACE VC in `viewChanges[]` whose lock is `(105, ph105)` with `lastLanded = (100, ph100)`: `ViewChangeMismatch`. (iv) L1-R4-01: `replaceableFrom = R`, flag unspent; at `R + 12` `recordViewChange(REPLACE, lock = lastLanded)` is accepted, the flag becomes consumed, `replaceableFrom` stays `R`, no `rfLog` write by the record; a replacement landing with `replace` equal to its `l1Ref` at `R + 240` passes L9; a C3-R07 A1-disjunct hatch at `R + 240` (no earlier hatch) passes check 1. Control: an announcement at `R − 100` spends the flag, `rfLog` is written, `replaceableFrom = R + 600`, and the same replacement at `R + 240` reverts `NotReplaceable`. Second control (L1-R5-01, red-team cycle 2 round 5, 2026-10-04): flag unspent, `replaceableFrom = R`; `announceLanding` in block A (timestamp ≤ R) spends the flag, writes `rfLog[A] = (A, prev, R)`, `replaceableFrom = R + 600`; at `R + 612` `recordViewChange(REPLACE, lock = lastLanded)` in block M is accepted, the flag stays spent, `replaceableFrom` stays `R + 600`, no `rfLog` write; a REPLACE lock vote at `l1Ref = (N, hash N)`, `A < N ≤ M`, timestamp `R + 300`, is premature (A1 false at N's start) and S3d evidence with `_rfCheckpoint = 0` (`rfLogHead = A < N`) reads the live `R + 600` and slashes (S3-V05, L1-R5-01). (v) L1-R4-02: `lastLanded = h`; A calls `announceLanding(h + 1, e)` (2,000 TAIKO locked); a conflict enters CONFLICT; `announceLanding(h + 1, e′)` by B reverts `NotActive`; the restart voids A's announcement and A's ledger regains 2,000 TAIKO; the first post-restart landing covering `h + 1` emits no `AnnouncementForfeited`. (vi) L1-R4-03: ZK leaves 0 and 1; record `k` landed by leaf 1 and confirmed by leaf 0, then finalized: `ProvisionalFinalized(k, 0)`; record `k′` landed by leaf 1, finalized by the time rule after 86,400 s: `ProvisionalFinalized(k′, 255)`. ZK_N = 3 delayed case (red-team cycle 2 round 5, L1-R5-02, 2026-10-04): leaves 0, 1 and 2; record `j` (leaf 0) PROVISIONAL; record `j + 1` landed by leaf 0 and confirmed by leaf 2 (`confirmingLeaf = 2` stored, no event); at `j`'s time-finalization the next pass emits `ProvisionalFinalized(j, 255)` then `ProvisionalFinalized(j + 1, 2)`, also when `j + 1` is by then older than 86,400 s. ## 15. Limits and rejected alternatives

| Choice | Reason and residual |
|---|---|
| One action, not propose then prove; no data-only landings in an outage | **Proven (C2-R04):** nothing on L1 references unproven data; a stopped prover voids nothing inside the horizon; data-only landings reintroduce the split. |
| Derived batch commitment, not a seal | **Proven (C2-R02):** no withholdable object on the landing path. |
| Landing-based arbitration, not a recorded REPLACE close | **Proven (C2-R09):** a closing record voided locked blocks with no proof (round 4, R4H-5, R4-C3). |
| No penalty for not landing; certificates verified on L1 | **Proven (C2-R07, L5):** a time rule cascades, an evidence rule has a guest bug as a false positive; in-guest certificates would rest the withholding defence on T3. |
| Two leaves degrading to a provisional one, not hard 2-of-2 or permanent single proof | **Assumed (C2-R12 to C2-R14):** hard 2-of-2 halts until a DAO upgrade; permanent single proof puts the ZK-bug case on attesters; an immediate one-leaf checkpoint was as final as two. Residual: L-PF, L19, L7; the mode opens whenever ZK_K proofs cannot be obtained for DEGRADE_AFTER, one withheld leaf included (B-C2-04). Residual added 2026-10-03 (finding landing-02): L-SPT, signed **[signed: D79 (1)]** (2026-10-04; earlier "proposed, not signed [open: user sign-off]"); the mode lands at most 240 blocks per DEGRADE_AFTER (MAX_VIEWS = 4 segments, one per occupied term, the occupied terms not necessarily consecutive; 240 s of chain only under full production; MAX_BLOCKS = 256 does not bind; corrected 2026-10-03, B-W22-A05, earlier "240 s of chain") (C2-R12), so during a one-back-end outage the certified backlog is replaceable from A1, depends on voluntary holders past RETAIN_SECONDS (L25), and is void at the landing horizon (about 4.7 h at 2-s L1 slots, about 28 h at 12-s slots, with every L1 slot filled and at full L2 production, C2-R12's full-cadence premise [assumed: premise; qualified 2026-10-03, the `ca4a942` follow-up Lows]; sparser L2 production lets a 240-block landing cover more elapsed time, so the horizon comes later or not at all). |
| Lander-chosen ranges, per-landing caps, per-recipient proofs | **Proven (C2-R01, C2-R02, C2-R04, C2-R16):** no slot coupling (R5); splitting earns nothing beyond ⌈bytesLanded / LANDING_UNIT⌉ landings; a swapped reward address cannot land a copied proof (round 2, S6), while anyone may submit a proof with its bound recipient (B-C2-01). |
| A derived identity `phHash` with the raw signature in the record, not the envelope and signature bytes committed into the identity | **Assumed (C2-R02's limitation, B-C2-02):** committing the witness would make the attested object depend on a value the sequencer alone chooses after attestation, reopening the identity question S2-R03 closed. Residual: blobs of one range are identical only up to the signature witness and the quorum witness bytes of the view changes they carry (D21, D23); two circulating witnesses may cost two proofs; the lander uses the witness it received. |
| No prover role, auction or L2-fee pool; no L1 publication duty; no calldata DA fallback | **Assumed:** an assignee is a liveness dependency, a fee pool a second treasury; a failure to serve is unprovable on L1 (L3); calldata is six times dearer and not FOCIL-protected. |
| The horizon as the one time-based void | **Assumed (C2-R10):** unbounded role history costs state every term; the EIP-2935 window is an L1 fact (L14). Open: whether 34 h is the right trade (landing §18). |
| Rollback floors as epochs in the checkpoint map, not an overwrite, a reset or an exemption | **Assumed (C2-R21):** the three alternatives J listed in its review of #22196 (2026-10-01) were not taken: restoring overwrite semantics reopens the same-height conflict C1-R06 exists to close; resetting the map under an authenticated transition loses the inherited records below the floor; exempting heights above the floor from C1-R06 leaves the rolled-back records readable. Residual: one walk per read, growing by one per DAO rollback; the rule has no reachable trigger while C2-R13 and C2-R15 stand. |
| A conflict defined over the journal's partition (fixed inputs equal, any derived output different, the queue checks of (c)), not over a named list of end fields | **Proven (C2-R04, C2-R14):** a named list misses every derived output it does not name, as `endParentPhHash` showed (B's W12 verdict on #22196, item 1); at `4aa0299` dropping the re-check of C3-R09 steps 3 and 4 would have let a sound leaf raise a false conflict through a chosen due answer, and under D41 the forced-inclusion fields are fixed inputs (C3-R06). Rejected: re-running every landing check on the counterproof, which would refuse an honest journal that fails a rights or count check the false record passed. Residual: a range the sound guest rejects outright has no counterproof (T11, L-PF). |
| `endParentPhHash` as typed `LandInput` calldata proven by the guest, not recomputed by the inbox | **Assumed (C2-R04, C2-R06):** the inbox holds no intermediate PH of a multi-block range, and recomputing one would put a PH preimage in calldata and a PH rebuild on L1; one word of calldata, proven, is cheaper. Residual: none, a wrong word has no valid proof. |
| One `startDigest` word in the provisional record, not every `lastLanded` field | **Assumed (C2-R13, C2-R14):** the restart needs the restored block's PH, parent PH and opening from storage (C4-R12), and one digest checked against a supplied preimage costs one word where the fields cost three. Residual: the record grows from six to seven words (§12). |

**[open: unmeasured numbers an implementation must measure first]** Mainnet gas of the RISC0 and SP1 leaves; the EIP-2537 aggregate path for 32 keys; the registry walk at launch size; the forced-inclusion walk; the full landing under the selected L1 fork's repricing (EIP-7709, EIP-8037); the guest cost of 64 KZG versioned hashes and a 4,096-header chain; block-proof and aggregation latency; the calldata of a maximal landing; a term's revenue and the TAIKO rate behind every reward constant (L15).

**[assumed: historical discrepancy inventory; current dispositions below]** The following numbered items preserve the audit trail. An old source discrepancy is not a second rule; the W15 table identifies each accepted resolution and each remaining owner duty.  (1) Blob usable bytes: "31 bytes per field element" versus 130,044 (§12). (2) The 5,760-s anchored-block span omits the LAND_CHAIN_GRACE deferral that C3's 6,720-s bound includes. (3) SETTLE_MAX = 4 is given on the slashing page but omitted from the parameters page. (4) The checkpoint write is "in the same transaction" (landing intro), after the proof (landing §3) and "before verifyProof" (Anchor page); they agree only as transaction order with revert rollback, which C2-R15 states. (5) `headerCore` field widths are not given (E02). (6) The page has L11 (proof) where the brief names L1 to L10. (7) Whether `Landed.rewardGwei` is the lander's total or the sum of all debits across settled terms is unstated. (8) Whether one landing pinning several terms collects several PIN_REWARDs is implied, not stated. (9) The Anchor page (bridge-migration §3) says the SignalService "checks none" of the checkpoint invariants; the deployed `saveCheckpoint` rejects zero hashes (`SS_INVALID_CHECKPOINT`, `SignalService.sol:176-177`) and checks nothing about heights; C2-R15 follows the code. (10) **Superseded.** Candidate A's parameters page says attesters "serve data until any landing is possible"; the normative duty is C2-R18's RETAIN_SECONDS = 7,200 s, above the derived 4,500-s floor, and the page's phrase is an erratum against that page, to be carried into C6's parameter index; the limitation past RETAIN_SECONDS stays as C2-R18 states it. J's review of #22196 (2026-10-01), J-2, found that "two normative pages state different duties for the same bytes"; the converged section is the normative text. (11) Landing §16 bounds the collusion finality lag by LAND_WINDOW_MAX + REPLACE_GRACE, omitting the deferral that C2-R08 and L3 ("up to 75 minutes with a landing announcement") include; §13 follows the latter. (12) Landing §7 says a confirmed record "becomes final, and its checkpoint is written", then that finalization passes records in order writing the start of the oldest unfinalized one; C2-R13 follows the in-order rule through the CONFIRMED/FINAL split. (13) The horizon void reads "first term older than the horizon" (landing §6) and "older than ROLE_HORIZON and never pinned, or anchor tip out of the window" (L14); C2-R10 and §11 follow L14. (14) **Closed.** The first draft of C2-R15 disclaimed C1-R06 while B's C1-R07 applies it to the same L1 `saveCheckpoint` call (J-1); C2-R15 now defers to C1-R06, proves the landing path never reaches its conflict or duplicate case, and C2-R21 carries the rollback obligation. (15) S2-R03 defines `carriedCertHash` as "the hash of the carried certificate" without naming the certificate's preimage (its fields, its domain tag, whether the bitmap and aggregate enter it); C2-R04's V3 clause (i) hashes that preimage (D23), so the guest's check and E11 are fixed only once S2 states it; owner S2, with C8 for the tag. (16) **Closed, then superseded by D41.** C3 at `4aa0299` listed `fiRunBefore` and `fiRunAfter` among the journal and `LandInput` fields it owned (C3-R09, D17); D41 withdraws the run, and C2-R04 and §10 carry C3 at `76c6150`'s fields instead, all fixed inputs. (17) **Open, from the hatch synchronization.** Statements that rest on C3 at `4aa0299` and that C3 §10's C2 row does not name: C2-R19's inherited-verdict run bound and §12's DRIFT_MAX (**closed** in the D63 follow-up revision: DRIFT_MAX = 0 per C3-R07 and C7-R09 at `0f0a2f0`, the 65 s withdrawn; C6's L39 states the same and is closed, D65); C2-R16's derivation of the uncounted FI bytes from C3-R08's run cap (request records now count toward V10, so the 40-record allowance is moot, the 12,500-byte figure not re-derived); C2-R08's announcement refusal keyed on the head entry's due time and its note on C3's 112-minute bound (due-ness is withdrawn by D39); §12's MAX_L1_HEADERS_PER_LANDING note on C3's bound. Owner: C2's next synchronization.

| Requirement | C2 contribution, not a whole-design pass |
|---|---|
| R1 | **Proven conditionally (C2-R01, C2-R12):** anyone lands; no prover whitelist; one back-end outage degrades, never halts, certified operation: the mode opens exactly when ZK_K proofs cannot be obtained for DEGRADE_AFTER, whichever condition failed (a leaf, the bytes, a certificate, L1 inclusion), one withheld leaf included, and the provisional record bounds the consequence (C2-R13, L-PF; B-C2-04). **Fails in two compound cases:** an uncertified regime plus a back-end outage (L19) and CONFLICT (L-PF), both DAO-only. **Qualified (2026-10-03, finding landing-02) [proven: arithmetic, C2-R12]:** "never halts" holds for landing, not for certified operation: single-proof mode lands at most 240 blocks per DEGRADE_AFTER (MAX_VIEWS = 4 segments, one per occupied term, the occupied terms not necessarily consecutive; 240 s of chain only under full production; MAX_BLOCKS = 256 does not bind; corrected 2026-10-03, B-W22-A05, earlier "240 s of chain"), so during a one-back-end outage the certified backlog is replaceable from A1, depends on voluntary holders past RETAIN_SECONDS (L25), and is void at the landing horizon (about 4.7 h at 2-s L1 slots, about 28 h at 12-s slots, with every L1 slot filled and at full L2 production, C2-R12's full-cadence premise [assumed: premise; qualified 2026-10-03, the `ca4a942` follow-up Lows]; sparser L2 production lets a 240-block landing cover more elapsed time, so the horizon comes later or not at all), without a slash. **[signed: D79 (1)]** L-SPT signed (D79 (1)): a third case outside the conditional pass, a signed exception to R1 while a proof system is down (2026-10-04; earlier "[open: user sign-off] ... Proposed limitation L-SPT, a third case outside the conditional pass, not signed; D73 (6) signed L19 and L-PF only", label unified 2026-10-03 on A's recheck, earlier "[open: user decision]"). Design round 2026-10-03: the split-timer candidate was found unsound as proposed (byte capacity about 0.154× production at V10's cap, and unattacked fixes for the ring restart, held money, stickiness and a CONFLICT front-run), so L-SPT stays the disclosure and the candidate is a research note ([single-proof split timer](../research/single-proof-split-timer.md), **[open: research]**). |
| R2 | **Proven (C2-R15):** one unchanged call; SignalService address unchanged; storage unchanged except C2-R21's rollback floors in the reserved gap; the live audit is C4's. |
| R3 | **Assumed (C2-R01, C2-R18, §13):** the lander is an unbonded role with no entry or exit, and no registry; anyone with the bytes and a proof lands; all landers offline leaves ranges CERTIFIED and voids nothing inside the horizon (failure profile, §13); the bonded roles are S1's. |
| R4 | **Not C2's:** landing is decoupled from the 1-s block cadence (lander-chosen ranges, C2-R01; no C2 value in slots, §12); the cadence itself is S2's. |
| R5 | **Proven (§12):** no C2 value in slots; the block-denominated windows are L1's own (C2-R10). |
| R6, R7 | **Proven for the landing path:** no accusation object (MISS is S2/S3's; anti-monopoly S1/S3's); one L1 action with explicit DA and the proof, finalizing at once in normal mode (C2-R01 to C2-R06). |
| D1, D2 | **Not C2's / assumed scope:** a landing is not the user confirmation, C2 only bounds when a "locked" block can be voided (C2-R09, C2-R10, C2-R14); sketch-level interfaces with every unmeasured value marked, vectors open. |

| Rule G at record admission and in the signing gate (S3 §15 item 18(g); added 2026-10-04) | **[open: proposal to B (S2-R09, S2-R10, S2-R13), with C8]** C7-R06's Rule G binds validity and, through the guest, landing; it does not bind `recordViewChange`, whose H and F admission is S2-R13's. Proposed: an H or F record is admitted only if G1 (the supplied 13-word lock PH body hashes to `lockPhHash` and names `closes` at `(t, v)`) or G2 (the supplied ten-word logical preimage of `X` hashes to `closes`, opens `(t, v)`, carries the same lock pair, and `X` itself is a REPLACE or RESUME base, or a hop's `closes` is `genesisOpening` with that hop's `(t, v) = (firstEtnaTerm, 0)` and its lock pair equal to C4's bootstrap lock pair (C4-R08), or `X` satisfies G1 or G2 in turn, each closed object of the run supplied with its full witness and verified under S2-R10 (i) to (viii) (committee and key authentication, the aggregate signature, the redraw proofs) unless a named authenticated record covers it with exactly equivalent checks, Rule G included, and a defined writer, at most V_MAX + 1 closing objects per term the run spans) holds; the cost of each uncached edge is **[open: unmeasured]**, and no hash-only or bounded admission cost is claimed (revised 2026-10-04 on B-G-03, which withdrew "the preimages of the whole run supplied, one keccak per hop, at most V_MAX hops per term": a logical preimage proves identity, not a valid witness); if S2-R13's records serve as that cache, Rule G's predicate and the record selector must agree **[open: the authenticated record interface, B with C8]**; C8 names the error. Without it a G-failing H record above `lastLanded` is head-compatible and occupies X's class-H slot, every other H then failing `ViewChangeMismatch`, and X stalls until a FALLBACK or recovery path (R14 interaction **[open]**). The same rule in S2-R09's signing gate is what lets S3b treat an out-of-X lock as evidence (S3-R08). Activation boundary (added 2026-10-04 in the verifier round) **[assumed: proposed rule; open: B, C8]**: Rule G binds L4 and R13 admission only for closings with `VC.t ≥ ACT_TERM`, with a pre-`ACT_TERM` TIMEOUT, TERM_END or FALLBACK hop a legacy base of the G2 walk, and every lock vote L4 and R13 read verifies under the domain S3-R08's selector names (`DOMAIN_LOCK_G` iff `t ≥ ACT_TERM`), so a legacy closing signed under the pre-upgrade gate is never made unlandable retroactively (C7-R06; C7 §11 item 28 (b)(8)). Also proposed to B (C7 §11 item 28 (b)(7)): S2-R15's L4c projection checks that `(VC.t, VC.v)` is the pair `X` opens, since Rule G refuses view-skipping closings (signed only by at least Q colluding keys) that L4c as proposed would land. Closure: S2's text, C8's error, and vectors extending S2-V25 and C7-T19. |

C2 today binds only `FallbackBelowCertificate`, so this is a new error-order binding and is not written here. C8-R07 cites `FallbackNotFinal` to S2-R14 (ii) by rule id only, with the same precedence open (C8 W15). Closure: L4's order and errors with fixtures S2-V23 and S2-V30; exact bytes stay a fixture obligation. |

## 17. Evidence and credit

**[assumed: sources]** Candidate A's branch `claude/beautiful-maxwell-8pyecj` at `b311d1d` (2026-10-01), `packages/protocol/docs/Etna/design/`: `landing.html` §1 to §18; `interfaces.html` §1, §3 to §5; `parameters.html` §1 to §3; `bridge-migration.html` §1 to §3, §5 (M3), §6, §8, §12, §13, §17; `limitations.html` L3, L6, L7, L11, L13, L14, L19, L20, L-PF; `roles.html` §2; `preconf.html` §7, §9; `slashing.html` (SETTLE_MAX); `arguments.html` (T2, T3). Rounds are cited per rule; the anchor-free revision is issue #22147 with taiko-geth#601; the revision after the #22188 review (fix pass 2026-10-01) covers provisional finality and the retention statement.

**[assumed: credit]** The one-action landing, derived batch commitment, ramp with the sequencer as reserve bidder, two-leaf verifier with degradation and landing-based arbitration are candidate A's (judge synthesis of its B4 round, revised through round 6). C2-R13, C2-R14 and C2-R18 answer candidate B's review of PR #22184 (a one-leaf checkpoint as final as a two-leaf one; no stated publication duty). The hashing convention, C1's pin and parser bounds and the current-root guard are B's (PR #22188); the landing-intent object (§10) and the encoding vectors (§14) are B's. The deferral of C2-R15 to C1-R06, the restart claim of C2-R14, C2-R21 and the retention erratum (§16 item 10) answer J's review of #22196 (2026-10-01), J-1 and J-2, under the arbiter's decisions. The V5 application in C2-R04 and C2-R19 (inherited verdicts and the forced batch's block 0, both withdrawn under D41; the view-254 skips) answers J's review of #22199 (2026-10-01), J-1 and J-3, under D14. The read rule of C2-R21 (last appended floor strictly below the queried height) and the limitation of C2-R14 (a rollback hides records, not the Bridge effects executed against them) answer B's P-B-C1-03 (#22195 at 75616d5), items (a) and (b); the floor vectors of E06 follow B's V12. The recipient binding of proof reuse (C2-R02), the weakening of encoding uniqueness to "identical up to the sequencer's signature witness" with its limitation and vector E09, and the opening condition of single-proof mode (C2-R12) answer B's W8 verdict on #22196 (2026-10-01), B-C2-01, B-C2-02 and B-C2-04, under the arbiter's dispositions; the exact guest rule list and the two predicates of C2-R04, and C2-R16's derivation from C3-R08's cap, follow D17; the FI replay obligation of C2-R14 is the cross-section obligation of the same verdict. The V3 clause of C2-R04 naming the inner field `C.phHash` (with the block's own `PH.vcHash` for the first two blocks under an opening, the journal's `parentPhHash` and `grandparentPhHash` for the ancestors at a range's start, with `endParentPhHash` stored by C2-R06, the genesis exception for view 0 of `t_0`, and the skip for FORCED, sentinel-255 and GENESIS_CHILD blocks), `fiDueAt` in the journal and L8, and "identical up to the signature witness and the quorum witness bytes of the view changes it carries" answer B's re-verdict on #22196 (2026-10-01, comment 5934018060) under D23; the logical id without `redraw` and `signerTerm` follows D21, and the drift split (withdrawn under D41), VALID_LANDED's L1 reference and the accuser-supplied evidence follow D22. The journal's partition into fixed inputs and derived outputs (C2-R04), the conflict predicate over it (C2-R14) with vector E12, and the typed calldata source of `endParentPhHash` answer B's W12 verdict on #22196 at `6e2b732` (2026-10-02, comment 5945458948), items 1 and 2: B found the High, the honest counterproof refused because a false `endParentPhHash` lay outside the three end fields the predicate let differ, and the Medium, the missing calldata source; the citations of C5-R12 in C2-R20 and of C1-R06 in C2-R21 and the restart list of C2-R14 answer the same verdict's follow-ups, the restart list taking its fields from A's verdict on C4 at `d1ebe1e` (#22205, comment 5945385606) and C3-R05. Decision-log entries: D9, D10, DL-1, DL-2, DL-3, D14, D17, D18, D21, D22, D23, D25, D28, D31; in the hatch synchronization D39, D41 (C3 at `76c6150`, its §10 C2 row), D51 and D55.
